dense output into C with stride512. Do not pass strided quadrants to this descriptor.
The seven standard products and signs are in the user-approved proposal below.
Use only one level. Exact workspace strategy is a board choice, all costs timed.

TB[4]=B,[10]=transpose(A); TT[4]=transpose(B),[10]=transpose(A); all [16]=C.
A physical transpose is T[k*512+i]=X[i*512+k], never an identity linear copy.

{"approved_proposal": "# Proposed extension: one-level FP32 Strassen\n\nStatus: approved by the user on 2026-09-25: \u201cAllow one-level Strassen alongside current methods.\u201d The running classical block retains its saved plan. No benchmark rule, numerical tolerance, power setting, or budget was changed.\n\nRecommendation: allow the existing 15-specialist board to consider one-level Strassen alongside classical GEMM. The board would still rank experiments and Astra would implement its choices. This adds an algorithm family; it does not select a candidate or promise a speedup.\n\nSplit each 512-square input into four 256-square blocks. Compute seven block products instead of the conventional eight, using FP32 throughout:\n\n```\nP1 = (A11 + A22) (B11 + B22)\nP2 = (A21 + A22) B11\nP3 = A11 (B12 - B22)\nP4 = A22 (B21 - B11)\nP5 = (A11 + A12) B22\nP6 = (A21 - A11) (B11 + B12)\nP7 = (A12 - A22) (B21 + B22)\n\nC11 = P1 + P4 - P5 + P7\nC12 = P3 + P5\nC21 = P2 + P4\nC22 = P1 - P2 + P3 + P6\n```\n\nThe subproducts require 117,440,512 scalar multiply-accumulate terms instead of 134,217,728: 12.5% fewer. Standard recombination adds 18 block additions/subtractions (1,179,648 scalar operations). Memory traffic, allocation, calls and rounding effects can erase the savings. This is an arithmetic count, not a measured runtime prediction. The algorithm and its weaker numerical stability are described in [Higham's primary analysis](https://nhigham.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/high90s.pdf), sections 2 and 4.\n\nApproved implementation boundary:\n\n- Only the n=512 path may use one decomposition level. Other sizes and allocation failure retain the existing correct path.\n- All block extraction, sums, subproducts, scratch allocation, recombination and cleanup stay inside timed gemm. No persistent buffers, result caching, external library, mixed precision, or benchmark changes.\n- The current generic SME fallback can compute 256-square subproducts. The prepared optimized LIBXSMM helpers cannot: their exported shapes are 512 or 512-by-32. If the board needs a 256-square primitive, prepare it from the same pinned LIBXSMM generator, with the same license, ABI, byte and correctness checks as existing imports. Do not pretend the current helpers support that shape.\n- Preserve tolerance 0.002. Check existing deterministic cases plus cancellation and scale-sensitive public cases before official timing. A mathematically valid identity can still fail FP32 accuracy.\n- Preserve the same source/power separation, ten paired report repetitions, frozen official hill and existing promotion gate. Use the existing per-block and per-call caps. No deeper recursion or Winograd schedule is included in this proposed first extension.\n\nAlternative: keep research limited to the current classical GEMM packing, transpose, cache and instruction-scheduling families. They remain available; the previous failures do not prove their search space is exhausted.\n\nApproval was requested and received because AGENTS.md says: \u201cThe user adjudicates decisions\u201d and reserves \u201cproduct or research direction\u201d changes for the user. Luna supplied the breadth analysis; the primary agent checked the arithmetic and prepared this bounded proposal. No performance evidence for Strassen on this workload exists yet.\n", "mapping": "# GPT-6 Luna audit: 256-square helper for one-level Strassen\n\nThe approved seven-product equations and four recombinations are algebraically valid. Signs and FP32 rounding need explicit checks in any implemented candidate. This note does not choose a schedule for the specialist board.\n\nThe helper descriptor must be M=N=K=256, lda=ldb=ldc=256, FP32 inputs/output/compute, alpha1, beta0, no transpose and no prefetch. Export from the pinned LIBXSMM revision, with fresh source and binary hashes. Check the actual exported parameter structure size and A/B/C offsets; existing 512 metadata alone is insufficient.\n\nFor a dense row-major product P=X@Y, pass Y as column-major A, X as column-major B, and P as output. This computes P-transpose through column-major views and yields row-major P in memory. A quadrant inside a 512-square matrix has stride512 and cannot be passed directly to a helper whose leading dimension is256. Form or copy both operands into dense 256-square workspaces. The beta0 result also needs dense storage: a C quadrant has stride512 and is not a valid direct destination.\n\nA feasible bounded-workspace option uses three 256-square FP32 arrays: left operand, right operand, and product (768 KiB total). Form each pair of operands, call the helper, then accumulate the product with the appropriate signs into the strided C quadrants. Reuse these buffers across seven products and initialize output before accumulation. Other schedules remain the board's choice. All copies, sums, allocation, helper calls, recombination, and cleanup remain in timed gemm. No persistent state or precomputed input is allowed.\n\nThe existing full512 and 512-by-32 primitives cannot substitute for a separately generated 256 helper. \"st1w\\t{ z21.s }, p0, [x9]\\n\"\n    \"st1w\\t{ z29.s }, p2, [x9, #0x1, mul vl]\\n\"\n    \"add\\tx9, x9, #0x800\\n\"\n    \"st1w\\t{ z22.s }, p0, [x9]\\n\"\n    \"st1w\\t{ z30.s }, p2, [x9, #0x1,  \"sub\\tx2, x2, #0xf80\\n\"\n    \"sub\\tx2, x2, #0xf, lsl #12\\n\"\n    \"add\\tx3, x26, #0x0\\n\"\n    \"add\\tx0, x0, #0x80\\n\"\n    \"sub\\tx6, x6, #0x20\\n\"\n    \"cbnz\\tx6,   \"cbnz\\tx7, Llibxsmm_64\\n\"\n    \"smstop\\n\"\n    \"mov\\tsp, x29\\n\"\n    \"ldp\\td8, d9, [sp, #0xb0]\\n\"\n    \"ldp\\td10, d11, [sp, #0xa0]\\n\"\n    \"ldp\\td12, d13, [sp, #0x90]\\n\"\n <stdlib.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n\n/* Transpose through ZA: contiguous row loads become contiguous column stores.\n * The heap layout remains at[k*n+i], exactly as consumed by sme_gemm. */\n__attribute__((target(\"sme\")))\n__arm_new(\"za\") __arm_locally_streaming\nstatic void sme_transpose(int n, const float *a, float *at) {\n    const int vl = (int)svcntsw();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; i += 2*vl) {\n        const int rows0 = n-i < vl ? n-i : vl;\n        const int rows1 = n-i-vl < vl ? n-i-vl : vl;\n        svbool_t pi0 = svwhilelt_b32(i, n);\n        svbool_t pi1 = svwhilelt_b32(i+vl, n);\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; k += 2*vl) {\n            const int cols0 = n-k < vl ? n-k : vl;\n            const int cols1 = n-k-vl < vl ? n-k-vl : vl;\n            svbool_t pk0 = svwhilelt_b32(k, n);\n            svbool_t pk1 = svwhilelt_b32(k+vl, n);\n            for (int r = 0; r < rows0; ++r) {\n                svld1_hor_za32(0, r, pk0, a+(size_t)(i+r)*n+k);\n                if (cols1 > 0)\n                    svld1_hor_za32(1, r, pk1, a+(size_t)(i+r)*n+k+vl);\n            }\n            for (int r = 0; r < rows1; ++r) {\n                svld1_hor_za32(2, r, pk0, a+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+k);\n                if (cols1 > 0)\n                    svld1_hor_za32(3, r, pk1, a+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+k+vl);\n            }\n            for (int s = 0; s < cols0; ++s) {\n                svst1_ver_za32(0, s, pi0, at+(size_t)(k+s)*n+i);\n                if (rows1 > 0)\n                    svst1_ver_za32(2, s, pi1, at+(size_t)(k+s)*n+i+vl);\n            }\n            for (int s = 0; s < cols1; ++s) {\n                svst1_ver_za32(1, s, pi0, at+(size_t)(k+vl+s)*n+i);\n                if (rows1 > 0)\n                    svst1_ver_za32(3, s, pi1, at+(size_t)(k+vl+s)*n+i+vl);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* CPU SME outer products: four ZA tiles hold a 2VL x 2VL output block. */\n__attribute__((target(\"sme\")))\n__arm_new(\"za\") __arm_locally_streaming\nstatic void sme_gemm(int n,const float *at,const float *b,float *c) {\n    const int vl=(int)svcntsw();\n    for(int i=0;i<n;i+=2*vl) {\n        svbool_t pi0=svwhilelt_b32(i,n),pi1=svwhilelt_b32(i+vl,n);\n        for(int j=0;j<n;j+=2*vl) {\n            svbool_t pj0=svwhilelt_b32(j,n),pj1=svwhilelt_b32(j+vl,n);\n            svzero_za();\n            for(int k=0;k<n;k++) {\n                svfloat32_t a0=svld1_f32(pi0,at+(size_t)k*n+i);\n                svfloat32_t a1=svld1_f32(pi1,at+(size_t)k*n+(i+vl<n?i+vl:i));\n                svfloat32_t b0=svld1_f32(pj0,b+(size_t)k*n+j);\n                svfloat32_t b1=svld1_f32(pj1,b+(size_t)k*n+(j+vl<n?j+vl:j));\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(0,pi0,pj0,a0,b0);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(1,pi0,pj1,a0,b1);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(2,pi1,pj0,a1,b0);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(3,pi1,pj1,a1,b1);\n            }\n            for(int r=0;r<vl && i+r<n;r++) {\n                svst1_hor_za32(0,r,pj0,c+(size_t)(i+r)*n+j);\n                if(j+vl<n) svst1_hor_za32(1,r,pj1,c+(size_t)(i+r)*n+j+vl);\n            }\n            for(int r=0;r<vl && i+vl+r<n;r++) {\n                svst1_hor_za32(2,r,pj0,c+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+j);\n                if(j+vl<n) svst1_hor_za32(3,r,pj1,c+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+j+vl);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nstatic void fallback_gemm(int n,const float *restrict A,const float *restrict B,float *restrict C) {\n    if(n<=0)return;\n    float *at=malloc((size_t)n*n*sizeof(float));\n    if(!at) {\n        for(int i=0;i<n;i++)for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {\n            float x=0;for(int k=0;k<n;k++)x+=A[(size_t)i*n+k]*B[(size_t)k*n+j];\n            C[(size_t)i*n+j]=x;\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n    sme_transpose(n, A, at);\n    sme_gemm(n,at,B,C);\n    free(at);\n}\n\nextern void sera_libxsmm_panel32(const void *parameters);\n\nvoid gemm(int n, const float *A, const float *B, float *C) {\n    if (n != 512) {\n        fallback_gemm(n, A, B, C);\n        return;\n    }\n    /* Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T. Argument offsets\n       are exported from the pinned LIBXSMM ABI; all other fields are zero. */\n    const void *parameters[22] = {0};\n    parameters[4] = B;\n    parameters[10] = A;\n    parameters[16] = C;\n    sera_libxsmm_512(parameters);\n}\n\n/* Optional generated 32-row primitive.
